Let's include a "waterfill spectral root" optimizer:
def waterfill_spectral_root(a_init, max_time, ...):
  a=init; S=sum(a)
  best=a,bestscore
  cap_factor adaptive start 0.5? But too large unstable. Use cap = qmax*(1 - μ) with μ = 0.1 decreasing? Or target cap = best_qmax*(1-0.005) once.
  Loop:
    q=conv(a); qmax=q.max()
    if qmax < best: update
    # choose cap = max(baseline, qmax - step) where step = max(0.05*qmax, (qmax - target_qmax)*0.2)
    target = clip_and_fill(q, cap)
    A=fft(a,K)
    B=fft(target,K)
    At = sqrt_spectrum(B, A)
    anew=real(ifft(At))[:n]
    anew=project_simplex(anew,S)
    score=conv(anew).max
    if score < best: accept, else maybe blend candidate = (1-α)a+α anew; project; score.
    If no improve for iterations, reduce step or random perturbation.

Project_simplex as in previous.

sqrt_spectrum function:
def sqrt_spectrum(B, A_ref, K):
  # B complex length K, conjugate symmetric ideally.
  half = K//2
  Bh = B[:half+1]
  Ah = A_ref[:half+1]
  At = np.sqrt(Bh)
  # choose sign: for each idx, if |At - Ah| > |-At - Ah| flip
  # Need vectorized: flip where np.abs(-At - Ah) < np.abs(At - Ah)
  At[np.abs(-At - Ah) < np.abs(At - Ah)] = -At[...]
  At[0] = abs(At[0]) # ensure real positive? B[0]=S^2 positive.
  # if K even, Nyquist freq component should be real to maintain conjugate symmetry. B[Nyq] real; sqrt can be imaginary. We want full A real? Actually a real if A[K-f]=conj(A[f]); Nyquist A[half] must be real. So set At[half] = np.sqrt(Bh[half].real) if half<len? Use real.
  if K %2==0:
    At[half] = np.sqrt(max(Bh[half].real,0.0)) + 0j
  # build full
  Af = np.empty(K, dtype=complex)
  Af[:half+1]=At
  Af[half+1:]=np.conj(At[1:half+1][::-1])? For K even, length half+1 includes Nyquist; Af[K-half:K]=conj(At[1:half+1][::-1])? Let's do standard: Af[:half+1]=At; Af[K-half:K]=np.conj(At[1:half+1][::-1]) if K even? For K=8 half=4, At[0:5]. Af[8-4:8]=Af[4:8]=conj(At[1:5][::-1]) sets Af[4]=conj(At[4]) (Nyq) overriding? We set Nyq real, okay. For odd K similar. Simpler:

    Af[:half+1]=At
    Af[half+1:] = np.conj(At[1:half+1][::-1])   # if K even, At[1:5] length4 -> Af[5:]=conj(At[4:1:-1]), okay Af[4] remains At[4].
    if K%2==0: Af[half]=At[half]
  Ensure Af[0] real positive.
  return Af

Pseudo for spectral_optimizer(a_init, max_time, target_score=None):
  n=len(a); S=1; K=pow2(2n-1); L=2n-1
  a=normalize(copy)
  best_a=a; best_score=score
  A=fft(a,K)
  step = 0.1; stall=0; lam=0.05
  while time<max_time:
    q=conv_from(A?) A*A
    qmax=q.max(); score=2n*qmax
    update best
    if score <= target: break
    # choose several lambdas/steps
    candidates=[]
    for frac in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01] (maybe adaptive):
        cap = max(qmax*(1-frac), best_qmax*(1-1e-5), min(q))
        target=waterfill_target(q, cap)
        B=fft(target,K)
        At=sqrt_spectrum_closest(B,A,K)
        cand=real(ifft(At))[:n]
        cand=project_simplex(cand,S)
        candidates.append(cand)
        # also blend At with A
        for alpha in [0.5,0.25]:
           Ab=(1-alpha)*A + alpha*At
           # enforce conjugate? A real sequence implies Hermitian; linear combo of Hermitian remains. At should be Hermitian. Add small.
           cand2=real(ifft(Ab))[:n]; project.
           candidates.append(cand2)
    # pick best by score (compute each)
    for cand in candidates: sc=score; if sc<best: accept
    if none:
       # try gradient peak step
       cand=scoring gradient
       if improve accept
       else: perturb
    adapt frac down if qmax near best.

Let's design code in detail.

Helper functions:

def pow2_size(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def clean_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def normalize_sum(a, S=1.0):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a) * (S / len(a))
    return a * (S / s)

def score_obj(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    n = a.size
    K = pow2_size(2*n - 1)
    A = fft(a, K)
    c = np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s*s))

But score_obj includes FFT; for optimized local, we may compute with K cached.

def conv_self(a, K=None):
    n=len(a)
    if K is None: K=pow2_size(2*n-1)
    A=fft(a,K)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]
    c[c < 0] = 0 if small? Use np.maximum(c,0).
    return c

def q_from_A(A,K,n):
    L=2*n-1
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c

def cross_corr(a, w, K):
    n=len(a)
    A=fft(a,K)
    B=fft(w,K)  # zero pads
    h=np.real(ifft(np.conj(A) * B))[:n]
    return h

Testing cross_corr sign: For a=[a0,a1], w=[q0,q1,q2], K=4 maybe; h=[a0w0+a1w1, a0w1+a1w2] correct.

def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    # clean nan
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    # If S small? 
    # Standard algorithm. Use copy? 
    x=v.copy()
    n=x.size
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    theta=(cum - S)/idx
    valid=theta <= u + 1e-15
    if not np.any(valid):
       # all below? Then average projection? For sum S maybe S > sum? Standard: rho=n, theta=(sum-S)/n, but if no valid then theta > u_1, still use max.
       rho=n
       theta=(cum[n-1]-S)/rho
    else:
       rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))+1
       theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)

If S negative? no.

def waterfill_target(q, cap):
    L=len(q); total=float(q.sum())
    cap=float(cap)
    qmin=float(q.min())
    if cap <= qmin:
        # no headroom; return uniform? maybe shift all to mean? return q
        return q.copy()
    over=q > cap
    if not np.any(over): return q.copy()
    target=q.copy()
    excess=float(np.sum(q[over]-cap))
    target[over]=cap
    if excess <= 1e-300: return target
    under=~over
    room=cap - target[under]
    rooms=float(room.sum())
    if rooms > 1e-300:
        target[under] += excess * (room / rooms)
    else:
        target += excess / L
    # Ensure nonnegative and sum
    target=np.maximum(target,0.0)
    st=float(target.sum())
    if st>0: target *= total/st
    return target

def fft_target(target,K): return fft(target,K) ; target length L < K, zero pad.

def sqrt_spectrum_closest(B, A_ref, K):
    half=K//2
    Bh=B[:half+1]
    Ah=A_ref[:half+1]
    # Handle zero: use complex sqrt
    # To avoid warnings, add small imaginary? np.sqrt(complex)
    At=np.sqrt(Bh.astype(np.complex128))
    # choose sign per freq
    flip = np.abs(-At - Ah) < np.abs(At - Ah)
    At[flip] = -At[flip]
    # frequency 0 real positive
    At[0] = np.sqrt(max(float(Bh[0].real),0.0)) + 0j
    if K %2 ==0:
       # Nyquist should be real for real output; B real approx
       val = Bh[half].real
       if val < 0: val = 0.0
       At[half] = np.sqrt(val) + 0j
    Af=np.empty(K, dtype=np.complex128)
    Af[:half+1]=At
    if half+1 <= K:
       Af[half+1:] = np.conj(At[1:half+1][::-1])
    # If K odd, half=floor; At length half+1; Af[half+1:] length K-half-1 = half, At[1:] length half okay.
    # ensure symmetry maybe
    Af[0]=complex(float(Af[0].real),0)
    return Af

def spectral_waterfill_candidate(a, A, q, qmax, frac, K, S=1.0):
   cap = qmax * (1.0 - frac)
   target = waterfill_target(q, cap)
   B = fft(target, K)
   At = sqrt_spectrum_closest(B, A, K)
   cand = np.real(ifft(At))[:len(a)]
   # If slight negatives, clip/normal
   cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
   sc = cand.sum()
   if sc <= 0: return None
   cand *= S/sc
   return cand

def peak_gradient_candidate(a, A, q, K, S, mode, p, tau, top_peak):
   n=len(a); L=2n-1; qmax=q.max(); qmean=q.sum()/L
   if mode=='top':
       # top lags weights: choose lags above threshold maybe top m or above mean+α
       # Use weights = max(q - threshold,0)
       threshold = qmax - 0.1*(qmax - qmean) ?
       w = np.maximum(q - (qmean + 0.5*(qmax-qmean)),0)
       if w.sum()==0: w = (q==qmax).astype(float)
   else softmax:
       tau_eff=tau*(qmax-qmean) if qmax>qmean else qmax
       scaled=(q - qmax)/tau_eff
       # avoid overflow/underflow: max scaled 0
       w=np.exp(scaled)  # max 1
       # add tiny uniform
       w += 1e-12
   else power:
       if p==1: w = (q > qmax - 0.25*(qmax-qmean)).astype(float)
       else:
          base = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
          # scale to [0,1]: base/base.max()
          base = base / qmax
          w = np.power(base, p-1)
   wsum=w.sum(); if small return None
   h=cross_corr(a,w,K)
   # We want decrease high q. For power gradient positive. Use h. If using entropy? no.
   # Normalize h to avoid huge exp: h_scale = max(abs(h)); if 0 return.
   h0 = h.mean() maybe subtract mean to keep sum? Multiplicative normalizes. Use center = h - h.mean() to reduce overall drift? But gradient for constrained objective should subtract mean in multiplicative? If we subtract mean, high relative low; good. Use hm = h - h.mean().
   # For peak weights, hm may have negative; exp(-eta * hm/scale) increases low part.
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(hm)))
   if scale<1e-300 return None
   candidates=[]
   for eta in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]:
      factor=np.exp(-eta*hm/scale)
      ax=a*factor
      ax=np.maximum(ax,0)
      s=ax.sum()
      if s<=0: continue
      ax*=S/s
      candidates.append(ax)
   return best after scoring outside? We'll compute inside maybe.

def random_spectral_candidate(a,A,K,S,eta):
   noise = eta * (rng.standard_normal(K)+1j*rng.standard_normal(K))
   An = A + noise
   # make Hermitian to keep real: An = hermitian_part(An)
   cand=real(ifft(An))[:n]
   cand=project_simplex(cand,S)
   return cand

def entropy_candidate(a,A,q,K,S,eta):

def upscale_sequence(a, new_n):

   if m == new_n: return a.copy()

   x_new = np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   f_old = a * m

def central_binomial_init(n):
   p=np.empty(n); p[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): p[i]=p[i-1]*(2*(i-1)+1)/(2*(i-1)+2)
   return normalize_sum(p,1.0)

Let's write candidate generation functions top-level:

def mixture_target(q, lam, total=None):
    if total is None: total=q.sum()
    flat=total/len(q)
    return (1-lam)*q + lam*flat

def candidate_from_target(a, A, target, K, S, method='sqrt', blend=0.0, use_phase=False, use_mag=False, rng=None):
   B=fft(target,K)
   At=sqrt_spectrum_closest(B,A,K)
   if method=='sqrt': Dir=At
   elif method=='mix': Dir=(1-blend)*A + blend*At
   elif method=='phase': Dir=np.abs(A)*np.exp(1j*np.angle(At))
   elif method=='mag': Dir=np.abs(At)*np.exp(1j*np.angle(A))
   # ensure Hermitian
   Dir=hermitian_part(Dir)
   cand=np.real(ifft(Dir))[:len(a)]
   # project
   cand=project_simplex(cand,S)
   return cand

def gradient_candidates(a, A, q, K, S, list_params):
   out=[]
   qmax=q.max(); qmean=q.sum()/len(q)
   # top weight
   thresh = qmean + 0.5*(qmax-qmean)
   w=np.maximum(q-thresh,0)
   if w.sum()>0: out.append(apply_multiplicative(a,w,eta=...,center=True))
   # softmax
   if qmax>qmean:
      tau=0.1*(qmax-qmean)+1e-15
      scaled=(q-qmax)/tau
      w=np.exp(scaled)
      out.append(apply...)
   # entropy log
   qsafe=np.maximum(q,1e-300); u=1/len(q)
   w=np.log(qsafe/u)
   for eta 0.5,1:
      apply
   return out

def optimize_sequence(a_init, max_time, target_score, rng, verbose_prefix=""):
    a=normalize_sum(a_init,1.0)
    n=a.size; K=pow2_size(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    A=fft(a,K); q=q_from_A(A,K,n); cmax=q.max(); score=2*n*cmax/(a.sum()**2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    stall=0; it=0
    # parameter schedules
    spectral_fracs = [0.1,0.05,0.02]
    spectral_lams = [0.1,0.05]
    grad_etop = [0.1,0.3,1.0]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if best_score <= target_score: break
       it +=1
       # if current not best? a should often be best or current mutated.
       # Ensure A,q correspond to a? We'll recompute if a changed? We'll maintain by accepting candidates and recomputing A,q. Good.
       # Generate candidates
       improved=False
       # spectral
       if it % 2 ==0 or stall>0: ... maybe every iter.
       # We'll create candidate_seq list, but to limit runtime, break after improve.
       # To avoid too many FFTs, do one spectral target per iteration, then gradient.
       # Try targets in order of adaptivity.
       # Choose frac based on stall: fracs = [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01][:1+min(4,stall//20)?]
       for frac in spectral_fracs:
          target=waterfill_target(q, qmax*(1-frac))
          B=fft(target,K); At=...
          for dir in [At, blend 0.5 At]:
             cand=...
             sc, q_c, A_c = compute_A_q(cand,K) # sum maybe 1
             if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                 accept; improved=True; break
          if improved: break
       if improved: stall=0; continue
       # mixture targets
       for lam in spectral_lams:
          target=mixture_target(q,lam)
          B=fft... At...
          cand... score
          if improve accept
       # gradient
       # Use top weights and entropy; compute h, generate etas
       qmax = q.max()
       thresh = 0.75*qmax + 0.25*qmean? Use above.
       w = ...
       h = cross_corr(a,w,K); center; for eta in [0.05,0.2,0.5,1]: cand=mul; score
       # entropy similarly maybe.
       if improved: stall=0; continue
       # random perturbation
       for sigma in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1]:
           An=A + sigma*(normal+ i normal) (scale by s.mean?) ; An=hermitian; cand=project(ifft(An)); score; if improve accept
       if improved: stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          # If stall high, accept slightly worse? Or move a to a random candidate to explore? Could do a = best_a + pert and continue; but keep best separate.
          # To avoid stuck, set a = project(best_a + noise) with small noise; recompute A,q.
          if stall % 20 ==0:
              noise = rng.normal(0, 0.01, n) * (a.mean()?; a sum1 -> std=1e-3?)
              a=project_simplex(best_a + noise,1.0)
              A=fft(a,K); q=q_from_A(A,K,n); qmax=q.max()
          if stall > 200:
              # restart from best with larger perturbation
              a=project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0,0.03,n),1.0)
              ...
    return best_a,best_score

def eval_candidate(cand,K):
   s=float(cand.sum())
   if s <=1e-15: return np.inf,None,None
   if abs(s-1.0)>1e-12:
      cand = cand*(1.0/s)
   A=fft(cand,K)
   q=q_from_A(A,K,cand.size)
   cmax=float(q.max())
   return 2*cand.size*cmax, q, A

def sqrt_spectrum_phase(A_ref,B,K):
   half=K//2; Bh=B[:half+1]; Ah=A_ref[:half+1]
   mag=np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.abs(Bh),0))
   theta=np.angle(Ah)
   err=np.angle(Bh) - 2*theta
   err= (err + np.pi)%(2*np.pi)-np.pi
   At=mag*np.exp(1j*(theta + 0.5*err))
   At[0]=abs(At[0])...
   sym. This is effectively signed sqrt with phase continuity; may be more stable. Use both.

def candidate_from_fft_direction(Dir,n,S,K):
   # Dir length K Hermitian approx
   Dir=hermitian_part(Dir)
   cand=np.real(ifft(Dir))[:n]
   cand=project_simplex(cand,S)
   return cand
def spectral_candidates_from_target(A,target,K,n,S):
   out_dirs=[]
   B=fft(target,K)
   At=sqrt_spectrum_closest(B,A,K)
   out_dirs.append(At)
   out_dirs.append(0.5*A+0.5*At)
   out_dirs.append(0.7*A+0.3*At)
   # phase-only and mag-only maybe optional
   angA=np.angle(A); magAt=np.abs(At)
   out_dirs.append(magAt*np.exp(1j*angA))
   # phase-only: magA*exp(i angle At)
   out_dirs.append(np.abs(A)*np.exp(1j*np.angle(At)))
   return [candidate_from_fft_direction(D,n,S,K) for D in out_dirs]

Random candidates:
def random_candidate(a,A,K,S,rng,sigma):
   scale=float(np.linalg.norm(A))/np.sqrt(K)
   noise=sigma*scale*(rng.standard_normal(K)+1j*rng.standard_normal(K))
   An=hermitian_part(A+noise)
   return candidate_from_fft_direction(An,K,S)

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   start=time.monotonic()
   deadline = start + max(10.0, float(budget_s)-20.0)
   global_best_a=None; global_best_score=np.inf

Pseudo optimize_sequence:

def optimize_sequence(a_init, max_time, target_score, rng, verbose_prefix=""):
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, 1.0)
    n = a.size; K = pow2_size(2*n - 1); L=2*n-1; S=1.0
    A = fft(a, K)
    q = q_from_A(A,K,n)
    qmax = float(q.max())
    best_score = float(2.0*n*qmax)  # since sum 1
    # ensure sum exactly: if not, use formula
    s=a.sum(); best_score = float(2.0*n*qmax/(s*s))
    best_a = a.copy()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, max_time)
    stall = 0; it=0
    print(...)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if best_score <= target_score: break
       qmax = float(q.max())
       # If several iterations no improve, reset a to best? We'll manage below.
       improved = False
       # spectral waterfill candidates
       fracs = [0.05, 0.02, 0.01]
       if stall > 30: fracs.append(0.1)
       if stall > 100: fracs.insert(0,0.08)
       for frac in fracs:
           target = waterfill_target(q, qmax*(1.0-frac))
           B = fft(target,K)
           At = sqrt_spectrum_closest(B,A,K)
           cand_dirs=[At, 0.6*A+0.4*At, 0.8*A+0.2*At]
           for D in cand_dirs:
               cand = candidate_from_direction(D,n,S,K)
               sc,qc,Ac = eval_candidate(cand,K)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                   accept; improved=True; break
           if improved: break
       if improved:
           stall=0
           if it % 100 ==0: print(...)
           continue
       # mixture targets
       for lam in [0.05, 0.02]:
           target=(1-lam)*q + lam*(q.sum()/L)
           B=fft(target,K);At=...
           for D in [At, 0.5*A+0.5*At]: ...
       # gradient top/entropy
       # We'll compute h for top weight once and try etas
       if not improved:
          qmean=float(q.sum())/L; qmaxq=qmax
          threshold = 0.75*qmaxq + 0.25*qmean
          w=np.maximum(q - threshold, 0.0)
          if w.sum() > 0:
             h=cross_corr(a,w,K); h=h-h.mean(); scale=...; for eta in [0.2,0.8,2.0]: cand=... score
          # entropy
          w=np.log(np.maximum(q,1e-300)/(1.0/L)); h=...; for eta in [0.5,1.5]
       if improved: stall=0; continue
       # random
       for sigma in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05]:
          cand=random_candidate(...); sc...
          if improve accept
       if improved:
          stall=0; continue
       # no improvement
       stall +=1
       # periodically reset current to best with small perturbation
       if stall >= 20:
           sigma_a = 0.01 / n  # absolute noise
           if stall >= 200:
               sigma_a = 0.05 / n
           a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma_a, n), S)
           A=fft(a,K); q=q_from_A(A,K,n)
           # If this perturbed current has better score, accept as best?
           sc=2n*q.max()/sum^2; if sc<best... (maybe)
       # If stall huge, try full best reset without noise
       if stall > 500:
           a=best_a.copy(); A=fft(a,K); q=q_from_A(A,K,n); stall=100
    print return
    return best_a,best_score

def project_simplex_fast(v,S):

       v=np.maximum(v,0); s=v.sum(); if s<=0: fallback sort; return v*S/s

Implementation:

def eval_candidate(cand, K):
    cand = clean_sequence(cand)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    n = cand.size
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf, None, None
    cand = cand * (1.0 / s)
    A = fft(cand, K)
    q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(q))
    sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)  # sum 1
    return sc, q, A

`hermitian_part` vectorized:
def hermitian_part(C):
   K=len(C)
   rev=np.empty_like(C)
   rev[0]=C[0]
   if K>1:
      rev[1:] = C[K-1:0:-1]
   H=0.5*(C+rev.conj())
   H[0]=H[0].real+0j
   if K%2==0:
      # make Nyquist real to help real inverse
      H[K//2]=H[K//2].real+0j
   return H
Check for K=8: C[K-1:0:-1] gives C7,C6,C5,C4,C3,C2,C1 length7. rev[1:]= that. Good.
For K odd 5: C4,C3,C2,C1. Good.

      v=np.maximum(v,0.0); s=float(v.sum()); if s > 0: return v*(S/s)
   # full projection
   return project_simplex(v,S)

def multiplicative_candidate_from_h(a,h,eta,S):
   h = h - h.mean()
   scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
   if scale <= 1e-300: return None
   ax = a * np.exp(-eta * h / scale)

   s=ax.sum()
   if s <= 0: return None
   return ax*(S/s)

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(a * a) / (sum(a))^2
where a * a is the full linear convolution.

The optimizer normalizes sum(a)=1 and works directly on the square
polynomial spectrum:
    a * a = ifft( FFT(a)^2 ) .

It combines:
1. A coarse-resolution basin search.
2. A spectral square-root water-filling update:
   - flatten the current convolution by clipping peaks and redistributing mass,
   - take a Hermitian square root of the target spectrum,
   - project the inverse transform onto the non-negative simplex.
3. Peak/entropy multiplicative gradient steps for local polish.
4. Random spectral resets to escape local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def pow2_size(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clean_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def normalize_sum(a, S=1.0):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, S / max(a.size, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= u + 1e-15
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
        theta = (cum[n - 1] - S) / rho
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
        theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def project_simplex_fast(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    minv = float(v.min())
    if minv >= -1e-12 * max(1.0, float(S)):
        p = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            return p * (S / s)
    return project_simplex(v, S)


def q_from_A(A, K, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def conv_self(a, K=None):
    n = a.size
    if K is None:
        K = pow2_size(2 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, K)
    return q_from_A(A, K, n)


def score_obj(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    K = pow2_size(2 * n - 1)
    A = fft(a, K)
    q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
    cmax = float(q.max())
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def eval_candidate(cand, K):
    cand = clean_sequence(cand)
    n = cand.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, None, None
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf, None, None
    cand = cand * (1.0 / s)
    A = fft(cand, K)
    q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
    cmax = float(q.max())
    sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return sc, q, A


def hermitian_part(C):
    K = len(C)
    rev = np.empty_like(C)
    rev[0] = C[0]
    if K > 1:
        rev[1:] = C[K - 1:0:-1]
    H = 0.5 * (C + rev.conj())
    H[0] = complex(float(H[0].real), 0.0)
    if K % 2 == 0:
        H[K // 2] = complex(float(H[K // 2].real), 0.0)
    return H


def waterfill_target(q, cap):
    L = len(q)
    total = float(q.sum())
    if L == 0 or total <= 0:
        return q.copy()
    cap = float(cap)
    qmin = float(q.min())
    if cap <= qmin:
        return q.copy()
    over = q > cap
    if not np.any(over):
        return q.copy()
    target = q.copy()
    excess = float(np.sum(q[over] - cap))
    target[over] = cap
    if excess > 1e-300:
        under = ~over
        room = cap - target[under]
        rooms = float(room.sum())
        if rooms > 1e-300:
            target[under] += excess * (room / rooms)
        else:
            target += excess / L
    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
    st = float(target.sum())
    if st > 1e-300:
        target *= total / st
    return target


def mixture_target(q, lam):
    L = len(q)
    total = float(q.sum())
    if L == 0 or total <= 0:
        return q.copy()
    lam = float(lam)
    lam = min(1.0, max(0.0, lam))
    return (1.0 - lam) * q + lam * (total / L)


def sqrt_spectrum_closest(B, Aref, K):
    half = K // 2
    Bh = B[:half + 1]
    Ah = Aref[:half + 1]

    At = np.sqrt(Bh.astype(np.complex128))

    flip = np.abs(-At - Ah) < np.abs(At - Ah)
    At[flip] = -At[flip]

    val0 = float(Bh[0].real)
    At[0] = complex(np.sqrt(max(val0, 0.0)), 0.0)

    if K % 2 == 0:
        valn = float(Bh[half].real)
        At[half] = complex(np.sqrt(max(valn, 0.0)), 0.0)

    Af = np.empty(K, dtype=np.complex128)
    Af[:half + 1] = At
    if K > half:
        Af[K - half:] = np.conj(At[1:half + 1][::-1])
    Af[0] = complex(float(Af[0].real), 0.0)
    if K % 2 == 0:
        Af[K // 2] = complex(float(Af[K // 2].real), 0.0)
    return Af


def candidate_from_direction(Dir, n, S, K):
    Dir = hermitian_part(Dir)
    cand = np.real(ifft(Dir))[:n]
    cand = project_simplex_fast(cand, S)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return cand * (S / s)


def directional_candidates(A, At, k):
    # Small, medium, and full spectral moves.
    out = []
    out.append((1.0 - 0.2) * A + 0.2 * At)
    out.append((1.0 - 0.5) * A + 0.5 * At)
    out.append(At)
    if k and k > 0:
        out.append((1.0 - 0.75) * A + 0.75 * At)
    return out


def random_spectral_candidate(a, A, K, S, sigma, rng):
    scale = float(np.linalg.norm(A)) / np.sqrt(max(K, 1))
    noise = sigma * scale * (rng.standard_normal(K) + 1j * rng.standard_normal(K))
    An = hermitian_part(A + noise)
    return candidate_from_direction(An, a.size, S, K)


def cross_corr_A(A, w, K, n):
    B = fft(w, K)
    h = np.real(ifft(np.conj(A) * B))[:n]
    return h


def multiplicative_candidate_from_h(a, h, eta, S):
    h = h - h.mean()
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if scale <= 1e-300:
        return None
    ax = a * np.exp(-eta * h / scale)
    ax = np.maximum(ax, 0.0)
    s = float(ax.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return ax * (S / s)


def central_binomial_init(n):
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * (2.0 * (i - 1) + 1.0) / (2.0 * (i - 1) + 2.0)
    return normalize_sum(p, 1.0)


def power_init(n, alpha=0.45, gamma=1.0):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + gamma
    a = i ** (-alpha)
    return normalize_sum(a, 1.0)


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    m = a.size
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if m == new_n:
        return normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    f_old = a * float(m)
    f_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old, left=f_old[0], right=f_old[-1])
    cand = f_new / float(new_n)
    return normalize_sum(cand, 1.0)


def optimize_sequence(a_init, max_time, target_score, rng, verbose_prefix=""):
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, 1.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), np.inf

    K = pow2_size(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    S = 1.0

    A = fft(a, K)
    q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    qmax = float(q.max())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * qmax / (s * s))
    best_a = a.copy()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(max_time))
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    if verbose_prefix:
        print("%s start n=%d score=%.7f target=%.7f" % (
            verbose_prefix, n, best_score, target_score))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if best_score <= target_score:
            break

        qmax = float(q.max())
        s = float(a.sum())
        improved = False

        # Desired per-coefficient peak implied by target_score.
        if target_score < np.inf and target_score > 0.0:
            desired_q = target_score / (2.0 * n)
        else:
            desired_q = 0.9 * qmax

        # ---------- 1) Spectral water-filling / square-root moves ----------
        if stall > 80:
            fracs = [0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01]
        elif stall > 30:
            fracs = [0.08, 0.04, 0.02]
        else:
            fracs = [0.05, 0.02, 0.01]

        for frac in fracs:
            cap = max(qmax * (1.0 - frac), desired_q * 1.001)
            if cap >= qmax:
                cap = qmax - max(1e-14 * qmax, 1e-18)
            target = waterfill_target(q, cap)
            B = fft(target, K)
            At = sqrt_spectrum_closest(B, A, K)

            moved = False
            for Dir in directional_candidates(A, At, stall > 60):
                cand = candidate_from_direction(Dir, n, S, K)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                sc, qc, Ac = eval_candidate(cand, K)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    a = cand
                    A = Ac
                    q = qc
                    qmax = float(q.max())
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True
                    moved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if it % 200 == 1:
                print("%s it=%d score=%.7f" % (verbose_prefix, it, best_score))
            continue

        # ---------- 2) Mild mixture toward uniform convolution ----------
        for lam in [0.04, 0.08]:
            target = mixture_target(q, lam)
            B = fft(target, K)
            At = sqrt_spectrum_closest(B, A, K)
            for Dir in directional_candidates(A, At, False):
                cand = candidate_from_direction(Dir, n, S, K)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                sc, qc, Ac = eval_candidate(cand, K)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    a = cand
                    A = Ac
                    q = qc
                    qmax = float(q.max())
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if it % 200 == 1:
                print("%s it=%d score=%.7f" % (verbose_prefix, it, best_score))
            continue

        # ---------- 3) Peak gradient multiplicative step ----------
        if not improved:
            qmean = float(q.sum()) / L
            threshold = 0.75 * qmax + 0.25 * qmean
            w = np.maximum(q - threshold, 0.0)
            if float(w.sum()) > 0.0:
                h = cross_corr_A(A, w, K, n)
                for eta in [0.3, 1.0, 3.0]:
                    cand = multiplicative_candidate_from_h(a, h, eta, S)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    sc, qc, Ac = eval_candidate(cand, K)
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                        a = cand
                        A = Ac
                        q = qc
                        qmax = float(q.max())
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

        # ---------- 4) Entropy-flattening multiplicative step ----------
        if not improved:
            u = 1.0 / L
            qsafe = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
            w = np.log(qsafe / u)
            h = cross_corr_A(A, w, K, n)
            for eta in [0.5, 1.5, 3.0]:
                cand = multiplicative_candidate_from_h(a, h, eta, S)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                sc, qc, Ac = eval_candidate(cand, K)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    a = cand
                    A = Ac
                    q = qc
                    qmax = float(q.max())
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if it % 200 == 1:
                print("%s it=%d score=%.7f" % (verbose_prefix, it, best_score))
            continue

        # ---------- 5) Random spectral perturbations ----------
        for sigma in [0.01, 0.03, 0.08]:
            cand = random_spectral_candidate(a, A, K, S, sigma, rng)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc, qc, Ac = eval_candidate(cand, K)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                a = cand
                A = Ac
                q = qc
                qmax = float(q.max())
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if it % 200 == 1:
                print("%s it=%d score=%.7f" % (verbose_prefix, it, best_score))
            continue

        # ---------- 6) No improvement: controlled restart ----------
        stall += 1
        if stall >= 20:
            abs_sigma = (0.02 if stall < 350 else 0.08) / float(n)
            a = project_simplex_fast(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, abs_sigma, n), S)
            A = fft(a, K)
            q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
            qmax = float(q.max())
            sc = float(2.0 * n * qmax)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0

        if stall > 1000:
            a = best_a.copy()
            A = fft(a, K)
            q = q_from_A(A, K, n)
            qmax = float(q.max())
            stall = 100

    if verbose_prefix:
        print("%s done n=%d score=%.7f" % (verbose_prefix, n, best_score))
    return best_a, best_score


def fallback_sequence():
    return central_binomial_init(2048)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)

    global_best_a = None
    global_best_score = np.inf

    def_time_left = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()

    # Try to import the already-found good sequence as a seed.
    height_arr = None
    try:
        x = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if x.size > 0 and float(x.sum()) > 0.0:
            height_arr = x
            sc = score_obj(x)
            if sc < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc
                global_best_a = normalize_sum(x, 1.0).copy()
            print("seed height_sequence_1: len=%d score=%.7f" % (x.size, sc))
    except Exception:
        height_arr = None

    def time_left():
        return float(deadline) - time.monotonic()

    # ---------------- Coarse basin search ----------------
    coarse_budget = min(150.0, max(0.0, time_left() * 0.07))
    if coarse_budget > 60.0:
        coarse_a = None
        coarse_score = np.inf

        # 256
        if time_left() > 70.0:
            inits = []
            if height_arr is not None:
                inits.append(resample_sequence(height_arr, 256))
            inits.append(central_binomial_init(256))
            inits.append(power_init(256, 0.45))

            best_init = None
            best_sc = np.inf
            for ini in inits:
                sc = score_obj(ini)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_init = ini
                    if sc < global_best_score:
                        global_best_score = sc
                        global_best_a = ini.copy()

            a_new, sc_new = optimize_sequence(
                best_init, min(60.0, time_left() - 5.0), 1.40, rng, "coarse256")
            if sc_new < coarse_score:
                coarse_score = sc_new
                coarse_a = a_new
            if sc_new < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc_new
                global_best_a = a_new.copy()

        # 512
        if time_left() > 80.0:
            inits = []
            if coarse_a is not None:
                inits.append(resample_sequence(coarse_a, 512))
            if height_arr is not None:
                inits.append(resample_sequence(height_arr, 512))
            inits.append(central_binomial_init(512))
            inits.append(power_init(512, 0.45))

            best_init = None
            best_sc = np.inf
            for ini in inits:
                sc = score_obj(ini)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_init = ini
                    if sc < global_best_score:
                        global_best_score = sc
                        global_best_a = ini.copy()

            a_new, sc_new = optimize_sequence(
                best_init, min(110.0, time_left() - 5.0), 1.40, rng, "coarse512")
            if sc_new < coarse_score:
                coarse_score = sc_new
                coarse_a = a_new
            if sc_new < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc_new
                global_best_a = a_new.copy()

    # ---------------- Fine n = 8192 ----------------
    n_fine = 8192
    inits_fine = []

    if height_arr is not None:
        inits_fine.append(resample_sequence(height_arr, n_fine))
    if global_best_a is not None and global_best_a.size != n_fine:
        inits_fine.append(resample_sequence(global_best_a, n_fine))
    if coarse_a is not None:
        inits_fine.append(resample_sequence(coarse_a, n_fine))
    inits_fine.append(central_binomial_init(n_fine))
    inits_fine.append(power_init(n_fine, 0.45))
    inits_fine.append(power_init(n_fine, 0.50))

    # Score and keep the few best distinct seeds.
    scored_fine = []
    used_msgs = []
    for ini in inits_fine:
        if ini.size != n_fine:
            ini = resample_sequence(ini, n_fine)
        sc = score_obj(ini)
        # crude dedup by first 16 values and length
        key = (n_fine, [round(float(v), 12) for v in ini[:16]])
        if key in used_msgs:
            continue
        used_msgs.append(key)
        scored_fine.append((sc, ini))
    scored_fine.sort(key=lambda zz: zz[0])

    rem = time_left()
    big_reserve = 0.0
    if rem > 1500.0 and global_best_score < 1.52:
        big_reserve = min(520.0, rem * 0.25)

    fine_budget = max(30.0, rem - big_reserve - 15.0)

    if scored_fine and fine_budget > 50.0:
        top_n = 3
        top = scored_fine[:top_n]
        per_init = min(300.0, fine_budget / float(len(top) + 1))

        for sc0, ini in top:
            if time_left() < 60.0:
                break
            budget = min(per_init, max(30.0, fine_budget - (len(top) - top.index((sc0, ini))) * per_init - 20.0))
            a_new, sc_new = optimize_sequence(
                ini, budget, 1.5005, rng, "fine_init")
            if sc_new < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc_new
                global_best_a = a_new.copy()
                print("fine init improved: n=%d score=%.7f" % (a_new.size, sc_new))

        # Long continuation from the currently best fine sequence.
        if time_left() > 120.0 and global_best_a is not None:
            if global_best_a.size != n_fine:
                start_fine = resample_sequence(global_best_a, n_fine)
            else:
                start_fine = global_best_a.copy()
            a_new, sc_new = optimize_sequence(
                start_fine, max(30.0, time_left() - 5.0), 1.5005, rng, "fine_long")
            if sc_new < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc_new
                global_best_a = a_new.copy()

    # ---------------- Optional longer sequence n = 16384 ----------------
    if time_left() > 350.0 and global_best_score < 1.51:
        n_big = 16384
        inits_big = []
        if global_best_a is not None:
            inits_big.append(resample_sequence(global_best_a, n_big))
        inits_big.append(central_binomial_init(n_big))
        inits_big.append(power_init(n_big, 0.45))

        scored_big = []
        used_msgs = []
        for ini in inits_big:
            if ini.size != n_big:
                ini = resample_sequence(ini, n_big)
            sc = score_obj(ini)
            key = (n_big, [round(float(v), 12) for v in ini[:16]])
            if key in used_msgs:
                continue
            used_msgs.append(key)
            scored_big.append((sc, ini))
        scored_big.sort(key=lambda zz: zz[0])

        if scored_big and time_left() > 200.0:
            # Use most of remaining time.
            budget = max(30.0, time_left() - 10.0)
            a_new, sc_new = optimize_sequence(
                scored_big[0][1], budget, 1.5000, rng, "big")
            if sc_new < global_best_score:
                global_best_score = sc_new
                global_best_a = a_new.copy()

    # ---------------- Finalize ----------------
    if global_best_a is None or global_best_a.size == 0:
        global_best_a = fallback_sequence()
        global_best_score = score_obj(global_best_a)

    final_a = normalize_sum(global_best_a, 1.0)
    final_a = clean_sequence(final_a)
    if final_a.size == 0 or float(final_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        final_a = fallback_sequence()

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]

    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            print("final evaluation rejected, using fallback")
            final_a = fallback_sequence()
            final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
        else:
            print("final verify score=%.7f length=%d internal=%.7f" % (
                val, len(final_list), global_best_score))
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("return length=%d internal_best=%.7f" % (len(final_list), global_best_score))
    return final_list
```
